1. `seed7-step5-mask-check` is empty (only `.` and `..`) - not usable

4. Reference config `_alfworld_seed300_step5_ood_eval_v3.yaml` is available

So per the instructions: "如果只有 high_kl checkpoint（qwen_exp029_seed7_high_kl_step5_hf）：这个 checkpoint 的 OOD 已在 Table 1 中（8.21%），但它是干预实验不是标准训练。

The high_kl step5 checkpoint already has OOD results in Table 1 at 8.21%, so re-running the evaluation would be redundant unless I need to verify or use it for a different purpose. But the instructions say I can use it for precollapse eval if needed, as long as I clearly mark it as the high_kl intervention version rather than the standard training checkpoint.

Looking more closely, the D134 protocol has different parameters than the reference config—specifically `enable_think: true` versus `enable_think: False`—so the previous 8.21% result might not be directly comparable if it was obtained under different settings. I should check whether the existing eval used the same protocol parameters before deciding whether to re-run it. Looking through the available checkpoints to see if there's a standard training seed7 variant I should be aware of.
